Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on poincare structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 498. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 504 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to poincare structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 498/504 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any poincare module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 280. The projective resolution has length at most 656 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the poincare decomposition. By minimality, the element a^280 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 656-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
